([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kendall-correlation-and-mse-computation))
- **kendall-correlation-regulatory-interaction-validation**: Use when validating single-cell multi-omics simulations where a ground-truth set of gene-peak regulatory interactions must be shown to exhibit correlations stronger than random non-regulatory feature pairs. - **kendall-rank-correlation-analysis**: Use when comparing per-cell pathogen (e.g. HIV or SARS-CoV-2) UMI counts from scPathoQuant's bbmap or bowtie2 aligners against CellRanger integrated-genome counts to assess cross-method agreement on 10x scRNA-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/kendall-rank-correlation-analysis--7c4173fbffe12372))
- **kendall-rank-correlation-benchmark-comparison**: Use when comparing pseudotime trajectory inference methods on a dataset whose true cell ordering (e.g., known developmental stages) is available and you need a rank-based ordering-accuracy metric per method.
